# RALF Training

This document claims end-to-end package training reproduction for CGL unconditional conditioning and records the completed label, label-size, completion, and refinement campaigns. The unconditional claim is supported by the staged validation ladder defined in [Validation Stages](#validation-stages), three package/original-code seed pairs trained for 30 epochs, and the original evaluator's test protocol. Each package mean fell inside the corresponding original-code three-seed range for all 15 metrics. An independent rerun of the full vendor-parity test suite recorded 28 passed tests, 0 failed tests, and 0 skipped tests. The scheduler milestone is epoch 21, derived as `0.7 × 30` epochs. PKU unconditional S0-S5 evidence and its final verdict are recorded below with status `s5-practical-reproduction`; PKU label S5 evidence and its final verdict are recorded in [PKU label S5 comparison](#pku-label-s5-comparison).

The CGL label-conditioned and label-size-conditioned paths have completed their staged validation ladders and full-run evaluations; their evidence is recorded in [CGL label S5 comparison](#cgl-label-s5-comparison) and [CGL label-size S5 comparison](#cgl-label-size-s5-comparison). The CGL completion path, completion (vendor task `partial`), in which the layout is generated given its first element, also has staged validation and a five-seed full-run comparison recorded in [CGL completion S5 comparison](#cgl-completion-s5-comparison). Refinement, in which a noisy layout is corrected into a clean one, has the same S0-S5 evidence shape and is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation; three of the 15 metrics fail the both-direction range test. CGL relation has accepted S0-S4 evidence and a completed 5+5 S5 comparison; its result and verdict are recorded in [CGL relation S5 comparison](#cgl-relation-s5-comparison). The both-direction range test requires each system mean to lie within the other system's observed minimum-to-maximum range. The PKU unconditional five-seed comparison and final verdict are recorded below, and the PKU label five-seed comparison and final verdict are recorded in [PKU label S5 comparison](#pku-label-s5-comparison). PKU completion now has staged and full-run evidence with a proposed status; its maintainer verdict is pending.

The PKU tokenizer has three labels (`logo`, `text`, `underlay`) and total vocabulary size `518`; CGL has four labels (`embellishment`, `logo`, `text`, `underlay`) and total vocabulary size `519`. The resulting one-row decoder vocabulary difference is `1,024` parameters (`519 - 518` times decoder width `1,024`), accounting for the S0 parameter counts `44,386,946` (CGL) versus `44,385,922` (PKU).

## CGL refinement-conditioned training

This section records the pinned-code S0-S4 ladder, the clean-target S1/S2 rerun, and the five-package/five-vendor S5 comparison. The S5 status is `s5-practical-reproduction`.

The pinned public code trains the noisy layout as both condition and target. The clean-target reproduction tests the authors' published behavior by applying a one-line harness patch to vendor training and using clean teacher-forcing targets in the package; [Observed vendor behavior](#observed-vendor-behavior) explains the mechanism and evidence before the stage tables.

### CGL refinement-conditioned stage evidence

The pinned public vendor code was used for the accepted S0-S4 ladder. Its artifacts record `source_commit` as `7eda783` before the rebase, now `e89cb9d` on this branch. The refinement recipe resolves to 35 epochs through `_recipe_epochs("cgl", "refinement")`, and the S3 record has the required natural and synchronized status object.

### Observed vendor behavior

At pinned vendor revision `c51db6032acbd0bd0ce72433becce08317e7874d`, refinement training uses the perturbed noisy layout as both condition and target. In `vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/helpers/task.py:152`, `get_condition` executes `batch[key] = new_batch[key].clone()`. Present since the vendor's first public commit `aae91db5f07976c9a8275dcef2dde41ba9ec1206`, the refinement branch draws CPU `torch.normal` noise in `GEO_KEYS` order, clamps it to `[0, 1]`, zeros padded elements, tokenizes `new_batch`, and performs that in-place assignment. `BaseAuxilaryTaskAutoreg.preprocess` in `vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/models/autoreg.py` then builds shifted targets from that mutated batch, while `RefinementPreprocessor.__call__` in `vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/models/layoutformerpp/task_preprocessor.py` consumes the same refinement condition for `seq_layout_const`. The real-batch evidence is `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/refinement/s1/final-002/s1.json` (SHA-256 `6576b3269f1d0b992db916a5739d383e9bcb54edaa9467f8da3ccad4e2358f29`), which records the clean and noisy layouts, vendor target token IDs, and `target_is_shifted_noisy_sequence: true`. Under the pinned code, vendor-seed-1 and package-seed-1..5 train condition copiers: their FID is approximately `5.0`, and their layout-only metrics are bit-identical at printed precision. The harness-side one-line patch `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/refinement/hypothesis/vendor-clean-target.patch` (SHA-256 `dd7de724ad19ac17bb80462c1d02c7d37a13ba0405b692bded5bfd1e425d6500`) removes only that assignment in the vendor working copy and restores the authors' published behavior. The reproduction target for this condition is therefore the authors' published behavior rather than the pinned public code as released. Evaluation is unaffected by this training-target choice: `vendor/ralf/scripts/run_job/eval_inference.sh:27-36` passes `cond_type=refinement test_split=test`, adds `sampling=deterministic` at lines 29-30, and then runs inference plus `eval.py` on the generated pickle files.

### CGL refinement S5 comparison

The question for this experiment is whether package refinement-conditioned training reproduces the authors' published behavior when the clean layout is teacher-forced and the noisy layout is supplied as the condition. The design used five matched package/vendor training seeds, the vendor's 35-epoch refinement recipe, the harness-side one-line vendor patch, the package clean-target path, the vendor inference path, and `eval.py` under the refinement condition. The result is that the clean-target reproduction is supported: vendor FID is `0.12564840864272733` to `0.13764282678530057`, package FID is `0.12638712407958033` to `0.16877349381038206`, and the authors' reference FID is `0.14299196227947655`; package/vendor means pass 12/15 both-direction range tests, with 0/15 Welch and 0/15 exact permutation p-values below `0.05`. The consequence is that the refinement comparison is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation even though three range tests fail and neither significance test separates the systems.

The package and patched-vendor runs used training seeds 1-5, batch size `32`, the recipe epoch count returned by `_recipe_epochs("cgl", "refinement")` (`35`), learning rate `1e-4`, gradient clipping `0.1`, AdamW weight decay `1e-4`, and the vendor scheduler. Vendor training used the harness-side clean-target patch and `++generator.auxilary_task=refinement`; package training used clean teacher-forcing targets with noisy condition kwargs. Evaluation used `cond_type=refinement`, `test_split=test`, `sampling=deterministic` as added by `vendor/ralf/scripts/run_job/eval_inference.sh:29-30`, vendor defaults `refine_mode=uniform`, `refine_offset_ratio=0.1`, and `refine_lambda=3.0`, inference seeds `0/1/2`, and all 15 values from `average.test`. The earlier top-k evaluations are retained under `superseded-top_k/` and are excluded. The both-direction range test means that the package mean lies within the vendor minimum-to-maximum range and the vendor mean lies within the package minimum-to-maximum range; Welch p is the two-sided unequal-variance test, and permutation p is the two-sided exact fraction of all `C(10,5)=252` relabelings whose absolute package-vendor mean difference is at least the observed difference, with means computed as `Fraction(str(value))` from the recorded decimal scores.

Both-direction range agreement passes for `12/15` metrics. The failures are `occlusion`, where the vendor mean is above the package range; `test_fid_layout`, where the package mean is above the vendor range; and `test_recall_layout`, where the vendor mean is above the package range. Welch p<0.05 occurs for `0/15` metrics, and exact permutation p<0.05 occurs for `0/15` metrics over `252` relabelings. The largest absolute standardized mean difference is `1.0400230478577683` for `test_recall_layout`.

The package condition defects were fixed in two steps. The condition-handling work is recorded by `3a2d05b` and finalized in the pinned-code source used for S0-S4 at `e89cb9d`; it added refinement to the shared full-condition path and made refinement preparation return a prepared batch without mutating its input. The clean-target fix is `8fca344`, with parity tests in `70d52dd`; it changed only the package target side, leaving noisy layout condition kwargs and the CPU noise stream unchanged. The harness-side plumbing is recorded by `27ac860`. Regression tests cover exact clean-target/noisy-condition parity and the no-mutation contract.

It is a single-run, 35-epoch reference only and is excluded from the package/vendor statistical test.

Verdict: Refinement-conditioned CGL training is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation. Under the clean-target reproduction, the one-line harness patch restores the authors' published behavior: 12 of 15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, no metric reaches p<0.05 under Welch or exact permutation, and the largest standardized difference is 1.04 on `test_recall_layout`. Under the pinned public code as released, both systems train condition copiers with FID approximately 5.0, which does not match the authors' published checkpoint with FID 0.143; the reproduction target for this condition is therefore the authors' published behavior.

Status: `s5-practical-reproduction`.

Run commands from the repository root. Generated data, logs, checkpoints, and downloaded assets remain under `.cache/ralf/` or the authoritative cache selected below and are not committed.

## CGL relation-conditioned training

The question for this experiment is whether relation-conditioned CGL package training reproduces the vendor's relation training within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation. The design used the accepted S0-S4 ladder, five matched package/vendor training seeds, the vendor's 50-epoch relation recipe, and the original relation evaluation protocol. The result is that 13/15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, with no Welch or exact permutation p-value below 0.05. The consequence is the verdict recorded in [CGL relation S5 comparison](#cgl-relation-s5-comparison).

### Relation condition and evaluation contract

During training, the pinned vendor's `helpers/task.py` `relation` branch calls `compute_relation`, which draws one Python stdlib `random.random()` value for each valid element pair to build `edge_indexes` and `edge_attributes`. Diffusion models consume those graph fields, but autoregressive RALF ignores them and takes relation tokens from the relationship table loaded by `RelationshipPreprocessor` at `vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/models/layoutformerpp/task_preprocessor.py:497-507`. The preprocessor shuffles each per-id relation list with `random.sample`, and each batch samples its relation subset with another `random.sample`. The relation sequence is therefore sampled rather than deterministic in isolation. Vendor `set_seed` seeds the stdlib stream. The package's `_consume_relation_graph_rng` advances the same stdlib draws without building the unused graph, then consumes the same list-shuffle and subset-selection calls in the same order and with the same sizes. The harness reseeds immediately before preprocessor construction and before each package/vendor batch condition. That is how S1 obtained exact relation token and pad-mask parity and zero per-token loss difference.

Evaluation used `vendor/ralf/scripts/run_job/eval_inference.sh` without a relation-specific extra flag. In `inference.py` around lines 326-336, backtracking is enabled when `cond_type=relation`. The authors' published relation output directory is `relation_name_top_k_temperature_1.0_top_k_5_final_dynamictopk_16_backtrack` and contains only the preview image `layout_test_0.png`, with no pickle or score files. This PR does not evaluate `gen_final_model.pt`.

The vendor `TrainingConfig` exposes `num_workers`, but relation inference's `TestConfig` does not and `inference.py` constructs its test loader with `num_workers=2 if not debug else 0`; `debug=True` also truncates the preloaded test split and is therefore not an equivalent protocol. The S5 evaluator kept `debug=False`, preload, the sequential sampler, and all sampling/backtracking settings unchanged, and used a launcher-only wrapper with `RALF_INFERENCE_NUM_WORKERS=0` to replace that loader argument. This is a scheduling-only setting: it moves batches from worker-backed shared memory into the evaluator process heap and does not change ordering or metrics. The override is recorded in each evaluation prelaunch record and manifest entry.

The relation recipe has no epoch override in `configs/ralf_cgl/relation.sh`, so `_recipe_epochs("cgl", "relation")` resolves to the base 50 epochs, with scheduler milestone `int(0.7 × 50) = 35`.

### Relation-table loader defect and fix

S5 exposed a packaging defect before the first epoch: the relationship-table pickle contains exactly three globals from the original implementation (`RelElement`, `RelLoc`, and `RelSize`), so `torch.load(..., weights_only=False)` attempted to import `image2layout`. The package does not depend on that checkout. It maps those three pinned enum globals to package-defined equivalents through a restricted unpickler and rejects every other global. The package S5 launcher runs without the vendor checkout on `PYTHONPATH`.

The post-fix S1 rerun loaded the real table with no `image2layout` module imported and produced relation-sequence SHA-256 `fbc7bc91012a092a239806524e9bed2777b7b0ff941736eca7517f3e4030d408`, identical pad-mask SHA-256 `71eeecf468dcfaebfa6f1a7dc05f98db13b6ecccadd71eac091ecf8297073b5e`, and 812 non-ignored per-token losses with maximum difference `0.0`. The aggregate scalar loss reduction differed by `4.76837158203125e-07`; the per-token loss vectors are byte-identical. Artifact: `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/relation/s1/loader-fix-001/s1.json`, SHA-256 `0fc3cc52a9b30351b4fa2a758fbfd7688156d1cdf9935954d7b632a1e85242cb`.

### CGL relation S5 comparison

Each system trained five matched seeds for 50 epochs with batch size `32`, training `num_workers=4`, and the relation recipe returned by `_recipe_epochs("cgl", "relation")`. Every final checkpoint was evaluated with the original `eval_inference.sh` protocol: no relation-specific launcher flag, `cond_type=relation`, `test_split=test`, default top-k sampling (`top_k=5`, temperature `1.0`), relation backtracking, and the output directory suffix `_backtrack`. The launcher-only inference override set dataloader `num_workers=0`; it changes scheduling and shared-memory placement only, preserving the sequential sampler and metrics. The comparison reads `average.test` from ten ledger-verified `scores_all.yaml` files.

The both-direction range test requires each system mean to lie within the other system's inclusive observed minimum-to-maximum range. Welch is the two-sided unequal-variance test. The standardized difference is `|Δmean| / sqrt((sd_p² + sd_v²)/2)`. Exact permutation p enumerates every `C(10,5)=252` pooled relabeling and uses `Fraction(str(value))` for the recorded decimal scores. Package and vendor means pass 13/15 range tests; no Welch or exact permutation p-value is below `0.05`. The largest standardized difference is `1.1190826789960107` on `alignment-LayoutGAN++` (Welch p `0.14350053110948044`, exact permutation p `38/252 = 0.15079365079365079`). Package and vendor mean FID are `0.587291940494553` and `0.5876304874301543`, respectively.

The ten launches used source commit `71c3242`. Later commits changed only typing, error handling, and documentation, so the trained artifacts are unaffected. Package seed 4 resumed after a host shared-memory fault from the complete Lightning checkpoint `epoch=48-step=74333.ckpt` (`epoch=48`, `global_step=74333`). Lightning epochs are 0-indexed, so 49 of 50 epochs were complete (`75850 / 50 = 1517` steps per epoch) and only Lightning epoch 49, the 50th epoch, remained. `train.log` shows only `Epoch 49` after the restore. The final checkpoint is `epoch=49-step=75850.ckpt`, SHA-256 `f6e7feeb0f7eeb9d586744b32c8348c378a6435a71d5e6032ba383d9dc57d5cf`; Lightning restored optimizer, scheduler, and loop state. The accepted S3 natural-trajectory nondeterminism caveat applies to this resumed continuation.

Verdict: Relation-conditioned CGL training is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation. 13/15 metrics pass the both-direction range test; the two misses are `alignment-LayoutGAN++` (standardized difference 1.1190826789960107, Welch p≈0.144, exact permutation p=38/252≈0.151) and `test_coverage_layout` (Welch p≈0.210, exact permutation p=50/252≈0.198). No metric reaches p<0.05 under either test. Layout FID is 0.587291940494553 (package) against 0.5876304874301543 (vendor), exact permutation p=250/252≈0.992. The authors' published checkpoint is listed as a reference row and is excluded from the package/vendor tests.

Status: `s5-practical-reproduction`.

## Install

```bash
uv sync --package ralf --extra training
```

Install the `vendor` extra only when rerunning the original-code parity checks.

```bash
uv sync --package ralf --extra training --extra vendor
```

## Data

The package-local CGL `RalfDataModule` and the original RALF loader consume the same authoritative cache for the accepted CGL staged evidence. The cache is provided read-only through the `RALF_CACHE_DIR` environment variable; its machine-specific mount path is intentionally not recorded in repository docs.

| Dataset | Source                                       | Config or path                                                                            |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| CGL     | RALF cache supplied through `RALF_CACHE_DIR` | `$RALF_CACHE_DIR/dataset/cgl`, CGL train/validation splits                                |
| PKU     | RALF cache supplied through `RALF_CACHE_DIR` | `$RALF_CACHE_DIR/dataset/pku`, exact package/vendor loader-stream evidence recorded above |

Its relevant contents include the CGL/PKU dataset trees, retrieval-index tables, the ResNet and FIDNet precomputed weights, DreamSim retrieval data, relationship tables, saliency/FAISS data, FID evaluation features, and the original training-log directories. It is an authoritative read-only reference for these runs. The cache is not localized into the repository worktree; the currently selected user-provided local copy is supplied through `RALF_CACHE_DIR`. Run provenance records the cache content-role inventory and a per-file SHA256 manifest; the external cache is identified only by `RALF_CACHE_DIR`, never by its machine-specific mount path.

The accepted CGL stream uses the transforms `image`, `sort_label`, and `sort_lexicographic`, fixed retrieval indexes with `top_k=16`, `random_retrieval=false`, and disjoint train/validation membership.

## Configs

Training configs live under `models/ralf/configs/training`.

| Config     | Dataset | Seed mode                              | Purpose                                                  |
| ---------- | ------- | -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `cgl.yaml` | CGL     | fixed seed, deterministic warning mode | Package-local Lightning training and CGL parity          |
| `pku.yaml` | PKU     | fixed seed, deterministic warning mode | Package-local training configuration; parity not yet run |

## Scheduler and Recipe Notes

The package training path uses `RalfTrainingModule` and `RalfDataModule` through the member-scoped `traingen fit` construction. The recipe uses AdamW, weight decay `1e-4`, gradient clipping at `0.1`, batch size `32`, and `accumulate_grad_batches=1`, so every batch reaches the optimizer directly. CGL uses a 30-epoch recipe. The multi-batch diagnostic described in [Validation Stages](#validation-stages) limits each epoch to three train batches and two validation batches; production training consumes the full loader.

The pinned original training command establishes the 30-epoch schedule through `vendor/ralf/scripts/train/ralf_cgl.sh:1-3`, which sources `vendor/ralf/scripts/run_job/end_to_end.sh:10-17,30-32`; that launcher sources `vendor/ralf/configs/ralf_cgl/uncond.sh:2-5`, where `training.epochs=30` is added, and `vendor/ralf/scripts/bin/train.sh:45-52` passes the Hydra override. The base `training.epochs=50` in `vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/config/__init__.py:14-30` is therefore not the schedule used for CGL. The package recipe uses 30 epochs, and `RalfTrainingModule.configure_optimizers()` derives the `MultiStepLR` milestone as `int(0.7 * epochs)`, giving epoch 21 without a hardcoded milestone. Both configs retain Lightning's `deterministic: warn` mode; the staged records establish the PyTorch warning state on the currently verified runtime.

### History / errata

Earlier records used 70 epochs and a 49-epoch scheduler milestone; those records remain historical provenance and are not evidence for the current 30-epoch claim.

### CGL label-conditioned recipe

The label recipe selects the original `c` task through `vendor/ralf/configs/ralf_cgl/c.sh:1-4`, which sets `++generator.auxilary_task=c` and does not override the base epoch count. The base RALF experiment at `vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/config/experiment/ralf.yaml:11-18` therefore supplies 50 epochs, batch size 32, learning rate `1e-4`, gradient clip norm `0.1`, and AdamW weight decay `1e-4`. Its scheduler override is `multi_step_lr` with `milestones: [0.7]` at `vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/config/scheduler/multi_step_lr.yaml:1-3`; `vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/schedulers/multi_step_lr.py:20-25` converts that fraction to epoch 35. The package harness passes `task=label`, `condition_type=label`, and the derived 50-epoch count while preserving those optimizer, batch, and scheduler settings.

The label condition shares the unconditional architecture, vocabulary, and seed-derived initialization; conditioning enters through the data/input pipeline rather than extra model modules. Consequently, the label initial state-dict digest is byte-identical to the accepted unconditional initial-state digest (`0c1c915b...`) while the scheduler milestone correctly changes from 21 to 35. The label canonical loader-stream digest is also identical to the unconditional digest (`664a4213...`), as expected because the loader stream is condition-agnostic; label construction occurs after loader output.

### CGL label-size-conditioned recipe

The label-size recipe selects the original `cwh` task through `vendor/ralf/configs/ralf_cgl/chw.sh:1-5`, which sets `++generator.auxilary_task=cwh` and overrides the base recipe to 40 epochs. The remaining recipe values are batch size 32, learning rate `1e-4`, gradient clip norm `0.1`, AdamW weight decay `1e-4`, and the `multi_step_lr` scheduler with milestone `int(0.7 × 40) = 28`. The package harness passes `task=label_size`, `condition_type=label_size`, and the derived 40-epoch count. The staged records use the same verified `torch 2.8.0+cu128` and CUDA 12.8 runtime as the accepted label campaign.

The label-size path shares the unconditional architecture and initial state-dict: its corrected S0 state SHA-256 is `0c1c915bc1d2a8e321869ad40b3f2bac0314c66a2b3a790f5c90bcb26bcbe88e`. Unlike the label condition, `cwh` changes the condition-token input pipeline and therefore requires its own S1-S4 evidence.

### CGL completion-conditioned recipe

The completion recipe selects the vendor `partial` task through `vendor/ralf/configs/ralf_cgl/partial.sh:1-5`, which sets `++generator.auxilary_task=partial` and inherits the 50-epoch base recipe. The package harness passes `task=completion`, `condition_type=completion`, and the epoch count returned by `_recipe_epochs("cgl", "completion")`, rather than hard-coding it. The remaining recipe values are batch size `32`, learning rate `1e-4`, gradient clip norm `0.1`, AdamW weight decay `1e-4`, and the `multi_step_lr` scheduler with milestone `int(0.7 × 50) = 35`. The vendor inference/evaluation condition is `partial`, as selected by `vendor/ralf/scripts/run_job/end_to_end.sh:34` and `vendor/ralf/scripts/eval_inference/eval_inference_all.sh:84-85`.

## Seed Policy

The staged checks use seed `1` on one selected Tesla V100-SXM2-32GB and are diagnostic evidence only. The full-run claim rests on its own seed scope: training seeds 1-3 on both systems, evaluated under the original protocol whose inference samples with the run configuration's evaluation seed.

## Validation Stages

| Stage | Scope                                      | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| S0    | Static config and initialized state parity | Confirms topology, parameter/state keys, optimizer groups, scheduler milestone, and encoded dataset state.                                                                                                     |
| S1    | Fixed-batch pre-optimizer trace parity     | Confirms prepared ids/labels, logits, and loss before optimizer mutation.                                                                                                                                      |
| S2    | One optimizer-step parity                  | Confirms named raw/clipped gradients, optimizer state, post-step parameters, and learning rates.                                                                                                               |
| S3    | Production multi-batch diagnostic          | Confirms production Trainer construction, scheduler/logging/accumulation/checkpoint wiring, with state-synchronized and natural trajectory layers kept separate.                                               |
| S4    | Deterministic loader stream                | Confirms train/validation order, transforms, masks, padding, retrieval tensors, and split membership.                                                                                                          |
| S5    | Full-run statistical comparison            | Each claimed condition uses matched package/vendor seed pairs trained under its pinned recipe and evaluated under the original evaluation protocol; condition-specific tables record the statistical evidence. |

The maintained lockstep diagnostic is `models/ralf/tests/vendor_parity/probe_s1_faithful.py`. It reproduces the natural S1 seeding placement and compares the package and reference condition construction, prepared inputs, logits, and losses; its recorded label result was byte-identical through loss after the harness began reseeding each system's stochastic preprocessing independently. The label 300-step real-scale probe used the production `traingen fit` path with the real batch size 32, six limited train batches per epoch, and the 50-epoch recipe (300 optimizer steps total). It matched 300/300 optimizer steps with `first_divergence=null`; the final trace is `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label/probe/runs/gate2-label-001/runs/run-002/trace/s3-trace.json` (SHA-256 `68d1af2686e9ab010e4e1e06dfee522c17e7c7a456fa9fef40353e352ecbecba`). The driver log SHA-256 is `275a5a40c5fcc9164d26f4f24e1ce4413d36fba013137f4de520884cf9d9ad8f`; final parameter and optimizer-state maximum absolute differences were `5.96e-8` and `2.04e-10`, respectively.

The S5 seed-1 pair completed. Both runs used 50 epochs, batch size 32, training `num_workers=4`, and `OMP_NUM_THREADS=4`; the vendor used `++generator.auxilary_task=c`, and the package used `condition_type=label`. Vendor final train/validation loss was `1.8892/2.3233`, package final train/validation loss was `1.850/2.303`. The checkpoint SHA-256 values are vendor `895cda39b4477d9e1d6e25f8e4eddc8dfe38b4a6839fd543d49f6bbb57025428` and package `d435e072ed7f70dbe3839e69aa6f24df47408c8dfaa8633503adcddc6723152d`. The seed-1 launch record is `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label/s5/seed-1-run-002/launch-manifest.txt`; the input package config SHA-256 is `2d4d5c6bc90ab06f53626ec0bc6e182ec882fff5d8aafaf5f31ba3aed49e167f`, the effective package config SHA-256 is `6142c24514bee3f2aad60ab2ef02869caa8917791a1fec69aacfd260db98b81d`, and the effective vendor config SHA-256 is `c519c83ad1b9f10a7f1eb11e165bf2816bbe33c310f6e4b0d6c18bb65f22b13d`.

Seeds 2 and 3 completed with the launch metadata and first-30-minute health evidence recorded in `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label/s5/seed-2-3-launch-manifest.txt`; seeds 4 and 5 completed with endpoint evidence in `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label/s5/seed-4-5-launch-manifest.txt`; all package and vendor endpoint and evaluation records are in `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label/s5/evaluation-ledger.txt`.

### CGL label-size-conditioned stage evidence

The label-size S0-S4 ladder used seed `1`, batch size `32`, the 40-epoch recipe, and the same verified `torch 2.8.0+cu128` and CUDA 12.8 environment as the accepted label campaign. A standalone CUDA measurement found same-system raw-gradient variation up to `1.3e-8` for vendor and `1.4e-8` for package, while the vendor-versus-package maximum was `2.3e-8`, the same order, in `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label_size/s3/backward-determinism/backward-determinism.json`. The audit interpreter path, torch version, and CUDA runtime are recorded in every machine-readable artifact and launch manifest; repository documentation keeps only the runtime values and uses `$RALF_AUDIT_VENV` for the interpreter path. S1 exposed and fixed a package cwh conditioning defect; the regression test is `test_label_size_condition_uses_per_sample_eos_and_padding`. S3 first exposed and fixed two harness memory issues (retained optimizer-state copies; cyclic autograd temporaries from the reference step that the Python garbage collector reclaimed too late), neither in the model or recipe, both absorbed within the memory budget by the label campaign; the regression tests are `test_optimizer_state_sync_does_not_retain_gpu_copies` and `test_s3_validation_cleanup_keeps_live_cuda_bytes_flat_across_cycles`.

### CGL label-size S5 comparison

The question for this experiment is whether package label-size training reproduces the vendor's label-size training. The design used five matched training seeds per system, the vendor's 40-epoch category, width, height conditioning (`cwh`) recipe, in which the layout is generated given each element's category and size, the vendor's own inference path, and the vendor's own evaluator. The result is that no metric separates the systems under the prespecified checks: 12/15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, 0/15 have Welch p<0.05, 0/15 have exact permutation p<0.05, and the largest standardized difference is 1.37 on `overlap-LayoutGAN++`. The maintainer assigns the reproduction verdict.

The package and vendor runs used training seeds 1-5, batch size 32, learning rate `1e-4`, gradient clipping `0.1`, AdamW weight decay `1e-4`, and scheduler milestone 28. Each final checkpoint used `cond_type=cwh`, test split `test`, `top_k=5`, temperature `1.0`, inference seeds `0/1/2`, and all 15 metrics from `average.test`; the table reports sample standard deviations. The S0-S5 evidence follows the [S0-S5 validation stages](#validation-stages). The both-direction range test means that the package mean lies within the vendor minimum-to-maximum range and the vendor mean lies within the package minimum-to-maximum range; Welch p is the two-sided unequal-variance test, and permutation p is the two-sided exact fraction of all `C(10,5)=252` relabelings whose absolute package-vendor mean difference is at least the observed difference.

| Metric                          |                             Package mean ± SD |                              Vendor mean ± SD | Range test |                Welch p |       Permutation p |
| ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------: | --------------------------------------------: | ---------- | ---------------------: | ------------------: |
| `R_{shm} (vgg distance)`        |      14.47579731303136 ± 0.011102450379249392 |       14.470769089038646 ± 0.0411679232692155 | pass       |     0.8034687395838606 |  0.8095238095238095 |
| `alignment-LayoutGAN++`         | 0.0024404333552099267 ± 4.414956228149606e-05 | 0.0023871153092177152 ± 4.382754545439011e-05 | pass       |     0.0916150815924124 | 0.10317460317460317 |
| `occlusion`                     |   0.12884223265984818 ± 0.0007169954290772816 |   0.12867589704818203 ± 0.0009258036592823367 | pass       |     0.7593954554215063 |  0.7857142857142857 |
| `overlap-LayoutGAN++`           |   0.39191062402120014 ± 0.0009550079174129694 |   0.39074437503733284 ± 0.0007331614022840333 | fail       |     0.0644472334832674 | 0.07142857142857142 |
| `overlay`                       | 0.005305804846713369 ± 0.00042905175541941583 | 0.0050832483136963435 ± 7.368744484046415e-05 | fail       |     0.3134425621973626 | 0.38095238095238093 |
| `test_coverage_layout`          |    0.9955459291347329 ± 0.0011254989796562749 |     0.995745862490281 ± 0.0008053828743439806 | pass       |     0.7557965868874279 |  0.7619047619047619 |
| `test_density_layout`           |    1.1011107408641565 ± 0.0025595648909901053 |     1.0987759635676997 ± 0.004628294711911934 | pass       |    0.36032006225044494 | 0.36507936507936506 |
| `test_fid_layout`               |      0.2375950704772663 ± 0.01918652415108446 |      0.2240645770101461 ± 0.01147179852893959 | pass       |    0.22085667463890407 | 0.20634920634920634 |

| `underlay_effectiveness_loose`  |    0.9846064874588227 ± 0.0018513134090038027 |    0.9853091971253047 ± 0.0011091450628136828 | pass       |    0.49178536284238067 | 0.49206349206349204 |
| `underlay_effectiveness_strict` |     0.9603078842163086 ± 0.002660232214935751 |    0.9611063321431477 ± 0.0022298100027215067 | pass       |     0.6213254420955404 |   0.626984126984127 |
| `unreadability`                 |  0.018593981351844945 ± 6.969691626483471e-05 |    0.018572026541803392 ± 7.8953614295672e-05 | pass       |     0.6537264117117899 |  0.6666666666666666 |

| `validity`                      |                      0.9986815169494466 ± 0.0 |                      0.9986815169494466 ± 0.0 | pass       | `null` (zero variance) |                 1.0 |

Both-direction range agreement passes for 12/15 metrics; the three failures are `overlap-LayoutGAN++` (both the package mean is outside the vendor range and the vendor mean is outside the package range), `overlay` (the package mean is outside the vendor range, while the vendor mean is inside the package range), and `test_recall_layout` (the package mean is outside the vendor range, while the vendor mean is inside the package range).

Welch p<0.05 occurs for 0/15 metrics, and exact permutation p<0.05 occurs for 0/15 metrics.

The largest absolute standardized mean difference is 1.37 for `overlap-LayoutGAN++`, using the mean difference divided by the root-mean-square of the two sample standard deviations.

Stage evidence: S3 required two harness-only memory fixes: after loading, the harness now drops the retained optimizer-state copy, and at each validation boundary it runs Python's garbage collector before releasing the CUDA cache so cyclic autograd temporaries from the reference step are reclaimed; neither fix changes the model or recipe. The evaluator now runs from a working directory whose cache link resolves to the vendor's precomputed weights; this is also an evaluation-harness fix.

Verdict: Label-size-conditioned CGL training is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation. Twelve of 15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, no metric reaches p<0.05 under Welch or exact permutation, the largest standardized difference is 1.37 on `overlap-LayoutGAN++` (p≈0.07), and the measured backward nondeterminism of the package matches the vendor's own (1e-8), so no implementation difference is indicated.

### CGL completion-conditioned stage evidence

The completion S0-S4 commands used the completion harness at commit `b53e1407172c6f0611e66a65056aec30f7e0963d`; the package source gate remained the full-layout fix at commit `68540939583ae644ce094bc13985881eb5e6883c`. This harness commit adds the `completion` CLI choice, maps it to vendor task `partial`, derives the 50-epoch recipe, uses a condition-specific S3 run base, and tests the faithful real-batch adapter. The real CGL batch check confirmed that vendor `partial` retains BOS plus the first `N=1` layout element and that package/vendor condition tensors and pad masks are identical. S1's `reduction="none"` loss vectors are identical over all 812 non-ignored tokens with maximum absolute difference `0.0`.

The package defect was that `_condition_kwargs` omitted `completion`, so completion training did not receive the full-layout condition even though the vendor `partial` preprocessor did. S1 comparison on the real CGL batch exposed the deviation; commit `68540939583ae644ce094bc13985881eb5e6883c` adds `completion` to the full-layout condition branch and a regression test. During S5, vendor training and evaluation also required a working directory containing `cache -> $RALF_CACHE_DIR`; the git-ignored S5 launch/evaluation harness scripts added preflight assertions for the CGL train and validation retrieval indexes and used that working directory. This was a harness-only fix and did not change package source or the training recipe.

### CGL completion S5 comparison

The question for this experiment is whether package completion-conditioned training, completion (vendor task `partial`), in which the layout is generated given its first element, reproduces the vendor's CGL `partial` training. The design used five matched training seeds per system, the vendor's 50-epoch `partial` recipe, the vendor's inference path with `cond_type=partial`, and the vendor's `eval.py` with `test_split=test`, `top_k=5`, temperature `1.0`, and inference seeds `0/1/2`. The result is that all 15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, 0/15 have Welch p<0.05, 0/15 have exact permutation p<0.05, and the largest standardized difference is `0.830001762680581` on `overlap-LayoutGAN++`. The consequence is that the completion comparison supports the verdict that completion-conditioned CGL training is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation.

The package and vendor runs used training seeds 1-5, batch size `32`, learning rate `1e-4`, gradient clipping `0.1`, AdamW weight decay `1e-4`, and scheduler milestone 35 from the vendor's 50-epoch recipe. Vendor training used `++generator.auxilary_task=partial`; evaluation used `cond_type=partial`, `test_split=test`, `top_k=5`, temperature `1.0`, inference seeds `0/1/2`, and all 15 metrics from `average.test`. Every checkpoint was evaluated through the vendor inference and `eval.py` path. The S0-S5 evidence follows the [S0-S5 validation stages](#validation-stages). The both-direction range test means that the package mean lies within the vendor minimum-to-maximum range and the vendor mean lies within the package minimum-to-maximum range; Welch p is the two-sided unequal-variance test, and permutation p is the two-sided exact fraction of all `C(10,5)=252` relabelings whose absolute package-vendor mean difference is at least the observed difference, with means computed as `Fraction(str(value))` from the recorded decimal scores.

| Metric                          |                             Package mean ± SD |                              Vendor mean ± SD | Range test |             Welch p |       Permutation p |
| ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------: | --------------------------------------------: | ---------- | ------------------: | ------------------: |
| `R_{shm} (vgg distance)`        |     14.349922499127828 ± 0.036053860632087265 |     14.374249492210108 ± 0.041167899629701334 | pass       |  0.3498094235288692 |  0.3492063492063492 |
| `alignment-LayoutGAN++`         | 0.0021310706533056705 ± 0.0001156976363891741 | 0.0021477523608018747 ± 8.415770016182541e-05 | pass       |  0.8015003041208134 |  0.8095238095238095 |
| `occlusion`                     |    0.12780161162537468 ± 0.001102518509587234 |   0.12806889603268573 ± 0.0006650210496432051 | pass       |   0.657499428449282 |  0.7222222222222222 |
| `overlap-LayoutGAN++`           |      0.389872184882985 ± 0.002808925902248135 |   0.38727847442868196 ± 0.0034118186877097733 | pass       | 0.22709586335484966 |  0.2857142857142857 |
| `overlay`                       |  0.005344526268972084 ± 0.0003031920520006708 | 0.005242153680086456 ± 0.00017705913089887692 | pass       |  0.5369669597170034 |  0.5476190476190477 |

Both-direction range agreement passes for `15/15` metrics. There are no range-test failures in either direction: every package mean is inside the vendor range, and every vendor mean is inside the package range. Welch p<0.05 occurs for `0/15` metrics, and exact permutation p<0.05 occurs for `0/15` metrics over `252` relabelings. The largest absolute standardized mean difference is `0.830001762680581` for `overlap-LayoutGAN++`. The permutation count for `test_precision_layout` is sensitive at the 16th decimal place because vendor seeds 3 and 4 have values `0.9611796067977342` and `0.961179606797734`: it is `198/252` under exact arithmetic, or `200/252` if those values are treated as tied, and this does not change any conclusion.

Verdict: Completion-conditioned CGL training is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation. All 15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, no metric reaches p<0.05 under Welch or exact permutation, the largest standardized difference is 0.83 on `overlap-LayoutGAN++`.

### CGL label S5 comparison

Validity is the fraction of generated elements whose decoded width times height exceeds `1e-3`. Each system trained five matched seeds for 50 epochs with batch size `32`, training `num_workers=4`, and `OMP_NUM_THREADS=4`; each final checkpoint used the original evaluation chain with `cond_type=c`, `test_split=test`, `top_k=5`, temperature `1.0`, inference seeds `0/1/2`, evaluator `--run-on-local`, and inference/evaluation `num_workers=0`. The primary table reads the `average.test` values from ten ledger-verified `scores_all.yaml` files. Package mean in vendor range means the package mean is within the vendor seed minimum and maximum; vendor mean in package range is the reverse check. Welch p is the two-sided unequal-variance t-test with five package and five vendor training seeds.

The n=3 comparison is retained as an interim finding because the maintainer directed the campaign to add seeds 4 and 5 before assigning a final verdict. At n=3, package means were inside vendor ranges for 11 of 15 metrics in both directions; FID, overlay, unreadability, and validity failed at least one direction. The n=5 table above is the primary result.

The evaluation campaign recorded two cgroup-v1 OOM kills when five RALF evaluator jobs loaded retrieval caches concurrently; the recorded cgroup peak was 482.611 GiB, and each retrieval-cache load used about 100 GiB. The campaign then capped concurrency at three RALF evaluator jobs and required `/dev/shm` free space of at least 100 GiB plus host `MemAvailable` of at least 80 GiB before each launch. Vendor seed 2 completed inference before its wrapper failed while reading an in-place launcher edit, so the evaluator ran once on the preserved nine-file generated-samples manifest (`1279e2246567ab4209f1b131ab2b28dac94a1099ecaf0cfbf015888874818f04`) and did not repeat inference. Package seed 1, package seed 2, and vendor seed 1 received post-hoc result-record repairs from their completed `scores_all.yaml` files; no inference was restarted for bookkeeping.

The authors' published reference is stored in the read-only RALF cache at `$RALF_CACHE_DIR/training_logs/ralf_c_cgl/generated_samples_c_name_top_k_temperature_1.0_top_k_5_final_dynamictopk_16/scores_all.yaml`, outside the repository `.cache/` tree. ### Control experiment: original vs original

The purpose is to establish the original implementation's own run-to-run noise floor under the exact both-direction range test and Welch protocol, so the label S5 result can be judged against how well the original reproduces itself.

Noise floor means the variation measured when the same original implementation is trained and evaluated again with the same recipe, while only the training seed changes. Vendor A is the original implementation with training seeds 1 through 5, and vendor B is the original implementation with training seeds 6 through 10.

For the both-direction range test, the first group's mean must be within the second group's inclusive minimum-to-maximum range, and the second group's mean must also be within the first group's range. Welch p is the two-sided unequal-variance t-test for the two seed groups. The permutation failure probability is the exact fraction of relabelings of the pooled values, preserving both group sizes, for which the same both-direction range test fails.

All five control trainings reached epoch 50/50 and all five original-protocol evaluations returned PASS. All five control runs completed without restart. The launch and result records, including checkpoint and score-file SHA-256 values, are in `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label/s5/control-vendor-seeds-6-10/launch-manifest.txt`.

The control analysis was recomputed from the fifteen ledger-verified score files. Its machine-readable and Markdown artifacts are `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label/s5/control-vendor-seeds-6-10/control-analysis.json` and `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/label/s5/control-vendor-seeds-6-10/control-analysis.md`; the Markdown artifact SHA-256 is `f39dbf5e6871a7ef76d833359520884fece21ecbaac2bf4b305d08c0dfe092a7`.

Summary: Package means inside pooled vendor ranges `15/15`; pooled vendor means inside Package ranges `13/15`; both-direction range agreement `13/15`; Welch `p < 0.05` for `0/15` metrics.

Verdict: Label-conditioned CGL training is reproduced within the vendor seed-to-seed variation. Vendor A versus vendor B has 13/15 both-direction range agreement and 0/15 Welch p-values below 0.05; package versus vendor B has 12/15 both-direction range agreement, with 14/15 package means inside the vendor-B ranges and 13/15 vendor-B means inside the package ranges, and 0/15 Welch p-values below 0.05. The one observation is that validity is lower across the five package seeds, with means `0.998568` versus `0.998710`, a difference of `1.4e-4`, or `2.05` standard errors; the exact permutation p-value is `0.070` over `3003` relabelings. No systematic cause was found.

### Validity investigation

The leading explanation is finite-seed trajectory variation acting on this rare event. A paired determinism-flag crossover would require 10 runs and about 240 GPU-hours, so it was judged not worth running because a `1e-4` validity shift would not change the verdict.

An invalid element fails the evaluator rule. Both systems were evaluated through the same vendor inference and evaluator: package Lightning checkpoints were converted to the vendor checkpoint format, then both systems used the same inference entrypoint and `eval.py` process.

The objective, tokenizer and vocabulary, optimizer groups and schedule, quantization and decoding, and data stream were verified identical. Same-checkpoint logits had maximum absolute difference `0.0`. The only training-path difference found was determinism handling: the package enables deterministic algorithms in warn-only mode, while the vendor enables cuDNN benchmarking. This can change numerical trajectories, but no direction for validity was demonstrated.

The invalid elements are positive tiny boxes created by the shared 128-bin quantization of ground-truth geometry. The raw data contains none; quantized targets contain 333, consisting of 237 embellishment, 90 text, 2 logo, and 4 underlay elements. The per-run count spread is consistent with binomial noise: the package sample standard deviation is `10.71` versus a binomial `11.12`, and the vendor values are `11.49` versus `10.55`.

The exact validity-only permutation test gives `0.070` over `3003` relabelings.

### S3 evidence and provenance

S3 reports both evidence layers in its machine-readable `status` object: `{"natural":"PASS"|"LEFT_CONTRACT","synchronized":"PASS"|"FAIL"|"NOT_RUN","verdict":"pass"|"bounded-pass"|"fail"}`. `LEFT_CONTRACT` means an independently initialized natural run diverged after the S0-S2 comparison checks; it is not a synchronized parity pass or failure. `bounded-pass` means the natural layer left those checks but the independent state-synchronized lockstep layer passed. Each per-run trace uses `PASS` for agreement, `LEFT_CONTRACT` for natural divergence, and `FAIL` for synchronized divergence.

Provenance for this rerun: S3 `s3/runs/harness-strengthening-002/` carries relevant source digest `939a47a9b963f2e4f5265a259868ac88ec665fd004c948e09409cd0c23a0c6fb`, matching the committed source at `a1c9e60`. S0/S1/S2/S4 were emitted from the pre-commit working tree; the subsequent delta was confined to S3-only functions, tests, and documentation. Their key values remain byte-identical to the accepted baseline records below: S0 `state_sha256` `0c1c915bc1d2a8e321869ad40b3f2bac0314c66a2b3a790f5c90bcb26bcbe88e`, and S4 package/vendor stream digests `664a421335f288c1a3beafc92f53d5d88ba685e5d393aea8fcc6eb484cdf3876`.

The fresh S3 per-run trace SHA-256 values are `9c1f4873b3ce0cc9c01b66e78ca407de40afb173a622b11666c955c8aae57c6c` (`run-007`, natural), `93a02eb72543e79db034a8028530c7e1a3bced62117af302a49d05fd4ba1bc46` (`run-008`, natural), and `1e864875a7a067492cc13c6eb01069021e3b8035fb244ebff008ca6c5fddfa61` (`run-009`, synchronized). The synchronized trace checked 90 optimizer steps, used scheduler milestone 21, and reported 2,658 storage entries with no shared storage at each post-copy check.

After the semantic blank-line change to the training-path modules, the staged checks were rerun under the protocol's [Regression Rule](../../docs/training-reproduction.md#regression-rule). Every rerun stage passed: S0 `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/s0-after-blank-lines-001/s0.json` (SHA-256 `466c427251545dae96b4aa9fea2de8bbcc3e391324cf70c0141ced9e4670730b`, initial-state digest identical to the accepted S0), S1 `s1-after-blank-lines-001/s1.json` (`ee29e6fc45068f3cb6a99b67de5d1d7c8c95ef5e5c7a50da2a1dafa4937e3b9b`), S2 `s2-after-blank-lines-001/s2.json` (`7d5ab67feda20b6d94280b6c4a6902c91f013f294d12dc6381e1065f1dc65480`), S4 `s4-after-blank-lines-002/s4.json` (`f4538ddd758ea75cb06638405b8f73721ac8e30187c548697ef25841791d9a45`, stream digest, checked batch count, checked sample count, and divergence fields identical to the accepted S4 run-010), and the S3 artifact in the table above.

The 300-step real-scale synchronized lockstep probe ran on one V100 at the recipe batch size 32, using 10 train batches per epoch over 30 epochs. All 300 steps record identical package and vendor state SHA-256 values with parameter and optimizer maximum absolute differences of `0.0` and `first_divergence=null`; peak allocated memory was 22.8 GB. The artifact is `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/probe/runs/after-blank-lines-001/trace/s3-trace.json` (SHA-256 `8a535a96272d0a1463358290868edd5fca4ec2f1a5b2f014a911c3abe4cf3f35`).

### PKU unconditional stage evidence

The PKU split was materialized into `$RALF_CACHE_DIR/dataset/pku` from an internally preprocessed copy produced with the vendor's hfds_builder pipeline; its ids were verified to match the vendor's `data_splits/splits/pku` lists and the cache's `pku_DATAID_TO_IDX.pt` exactly (train 7,734 / val 1,000 / test 1,000 / with_no_annotation 905). The package and vendor loaders read `image` and `saliency` columns from the parquet rows, so no separate image directory was required. The currently verified setup is GPU 6 with the cu128 interpreter, PyTorch `2.8.0+cu128`, and `PARITY_REQUIRE=1`.

The PKU first-condition harness uses `--dataset pku`. `_recipe_epochs("pku", "unconditional")` resolves to 50 epochs because `vendor/ralf/configs/ralf_pku/uncond.sh:1-5` selects the `uncond` task without overriding the base schedule. `RalfDataModule` maps names beginning with `cgl` to `cgl` and all other names, including `pku`, to `pku` in `models/ralf/src/ralf/training/datamodule.py:445-447`. PKU has three labels (`logo`, `text`, `underlay`) and total vocabulary size `518`; CGL has four labels (`embellishment`, `logo`, `text`, `underlay`) and total vocabulary size `519`. The resulting one-row decoder vocabulary difference is `1,024` parameters, accounting for `44,385,922` PKU parameters versus `44,386,946` CGL parameters. The S1 artifact records the prepared ids/labels and logits; the scalar loss is a float32 reduction, while the unreduced non-padding vectors are compared within the S1 tolerance (`rtol=1e-5`, `atol=1e-6`), with an observed maximum difference of `0.0` for this run, and averaged in float64.

S5 tooling was launched and is retained. It uses vendor inference followed by `eval.py`, `dataset_path=$RALF_CACHE_DIR/dataset/pku`, pku10 FIDNet weights, `cond_type=uncond`, top-k `5`, temperature `1.0`, and inference seeds `0/1/2`. The dry run verified all four PKU retrieval indexes, the evaluator cache link, config fields `dataset.name == pku` and `auxilary_task == uncond`, the 50-epoch recipe, one persisted failure notice per run/class, and fresh-start fixed-GPU queue fill. The evaluator launcher pins its inference DataLoader to `num_workers=0` so the preload pass is sequential; this is a scheduling-only setting and preserves the sampler/output path, while package training remains at `num_workers=4`.

### PKU label-size stage evidence

PKU10 names the vendor's `ralf_cwh_pku10` training and evaluation recipe; `hfds_builder` is the vendor's dataset-preparation pipeline. The PKU data was materialized from an internally preprocessed copy produced with the vendor's hfds_builder pipeline; ids verified against `data_splits/splits/pku` and `pku_DATAID_TO_IDX.pt`. The authors' `ralf_cwh_pku10/config.yaml` matches the 50-epoch, batch-32, `auxilary_task=cwh` recipe. The package calls this condition `label_size`; the vendor calls it `cwh`.

The audited cu128 runtime here means the Python environment used for these runs with PyTorch `2.8.0+cu128`; it is an observed setup, not a package requirement. `LEFT_CONTRACT` means the natural trajectory left the step-level numerical contract.

The S0-S4 artifacts and package seed 1 identify package source commit `8f870a3fd9ed8211e1adbf0d1042c2e23a97cd63`. Package seeds 2-5, all vendor seeds, and all ten evaluation source gates identify `c1a6ed29b76a3b119b9fa222e2f7267e86ee8503`, the pre-rebase launch commit, which is not an ancestor of this head. The code-only diff from that launch commit to this head has nine package and harness files, with 669 insertions and 73 deletions, from the merged CGL relation and PKU label work. Those changes do not affect `cwh`: `RalfTaskPreprocessor.__call__` routes only task `c` through `_label_sequence`; `cwh` uses `_geo_sequence`, whose shuffle calls are limited to `relation` and `c`. The changed CPU `randperm` is in the non-`c` branch of `_shuffle_seq_vars`, which the `cwh` path does not call. Relationship-table loading in `_load_context` and relation RNG consumption in `_run_s3_fit` are relation-specific; `RalfTrainingModule._condition_kwargs` guards its relation RNG and sample-ID branches with `self.condition_type == "relation"`.

The real-scale lockstep probe was required before the first S5 launch, but its pre-launch attempt was interrupted: it ended with `KeyboardInterrupt` inside `_run_s3_fit` 21 seconds before the first full-run launch and produced no complete trace. A post-hoc run at the pre-rebase launch commit `c1a6ed29b76a3b119b9fa222e2f7267e86ee8503` completed 300 synchronized optimizer steps over 50 epochs with training seed 1 (`status=PASS`, `first_divergence=null`). An empty `run-001` directory from the aborted first start precedes the completed `run-002` probe run. The complete trace contains 300 unique step records and is `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/pku/label-size/probe/runs/gate2-label-size-posthoc-001/runs/run-002/trace/s3-trace.json`, SHA-256 `a7047fd00bc7f5a35885e3461043b8ecfff192a5f9b18c6b2bda2190a9c0126b`. The driver exited afterward with `ValueError` while normalizing the trace path because the cache output was outside the detached root; the trace had already been written. The deviation from the pre-launch ordering is recorded in the [issue amendment comment](https://github.com/creative-graphic-design/design-generators/issues/308#issuecomment-5959929290).

### PKU refinement stage evidence

PKU10 denotes the authors' ten-category PKU configuration (`dataset=pku10`, `dataset.name=pku`). The audited cu128 runtime is the environment recorded in S0 with CUDA runtime `12.8` and PyTorch `2.8.0+cu128`. `LEFT_CONTRACT` means the natural repeated-step trajectory left the numerical contract established by the single-step checks; the synchronized comparison uses that same contract. The vendor's `hfds_builder` is its Hugging Face Datasets builder pipeline. The PKU10 split was materialized from an internally preprocessed copy produced with the vendor's hfds_builder pipeline; ids verified against `data_splits/splits/pku` and `pku_DATAID_TO_IDX.pt`.

The reproduction target is the authors' published clean-target behavior: the model receives a noisy layout as its refinement condition and trains against clean layout tokens. The pinned code trains noisy-to-noisy; deleting `batch[key] = new_batch[key].clone()` from the copied vendor `image2layout/train/helpers/task.py` restores the published behavior, as documented in the [CGL refinement section](#cgl-refinement-conditioned-training). The authors' [PKU refinement config](../../vendor/ralf/configs/ralf_pku/refinement.sh) sets `auxilary_task=refinement`; the authors' run config at `$RALF_CACHE_DIR/training_logs/ralf_refinement_pku10/config.yaml` matches the recipe with 50 epochs, batch size `32`, and the refinement auxiliary task.

Each final checkpoint used the original PKU10 refinement evaluation chain with `sampling=deterministic`, not top-k, `refine_mode=uniform`, offset `0.1`, lambda `3.0`, inference seeds `0/1/2`, evaluator `--run-on-local`, and inference/evaluation `num_workers=0`. Training used seed `1` for the staged checks, batch size `32`, and the audited cu128 runtime.

# Probe `run-001` failed at `S3.epoch[0].batch[0].loss` (expected `6.268060684204102`, got `6.264205455780029`) because it used the unpatched `vendor/ralf` tree; `run-002` used the patched vendor-workdir copy and passed all 300 steps. The passing probe used the production trainer at batch size `32`, six train batches per epoch, and the 50-epoch recipe. Its trace at `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/pku/refinement/probe/runs/gate2-refinement-001/runs/run-002/trace/s3-trace.json` has SHA-256 `cc127cdfea07efa318398215d8f6928087ccec8ec5acac280d1d6115684f38ce`; all optimizer steps and train batches completed with `first_divergence=null`.

### PKU completion-conditioned training

PKU10 is the authors' ten-category PKU setting (`dataset=pku10`; [vendor dataset config](../../vendor/ralf/image2layout/train/config/dataset.py)). `hfds_builder` is the vendor's Hugging Face dataset-building pipeline ([source](../../vendor/ralf/image2layout/hfds_builder/)). Dataset provenance: materialized from an internally preprocessed copy produced with the vendor's hfds_builder pipeline; ids verified against `data_splits/splits/pku` and `pku_DATAID_TO_IDX.pt`.

The authors call the completion condition `partial`; the package's `completion` condition maps to that vendor task, selected by [`partial.sh`](../../vendor/ralf/configs/ralf_pku/partial.sh). The authors' configuration at `$RALF_CACHE_DIR/training_logs/ralf_partial_pku10/config.yaml` matches the recipe (epochs 50, batch_size 32, auxilary_task partial). The recipe uses learning rate `1e-4`, gradient clip norm `0.1`, AdamW weight decay `1e-4`, and scheduler milestone epoch 35. The audited cu128 runtime means the interpreter supplied through `RALF_AUDIT_VENV` running PyTorch `2.8.0+cu128`.

All S0-S4 records below were produced at package commit `8f870a3fd9ed8211e1adbf0d1042c2e23a97cd63`, the merged PKU unconditional commit and an ancestor of this PR head. The only 300-step real-scale lockstep probe attempt is `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/pku/completion/probe/runs/gate2-completion-001/runs/run-001`: its intermediate `trace/s3-partial-trace.json` remains `IN_PROGRESS`, and its final `trace/s3-trace.json` reports `PASS`, 300/300 optimizer steps, and `first_divergence=null`. There were no other failed or aborted probe directories.

The run-local source-gate records for all five package training seeds, all five vendor training seeds, all ten evaluation gates, and all ten conversion gates report actual `source_commit=adc5d8efe8bb47880adf7d823f35ea93786e061c`. The 30 gate records show matching expected and found commits and contain zero source-check errors or tracebacks. These values come from each run's own gate record, not the launch-manifest expectation. `adc5d8e` is not an ancestor of this PR head, and no remote branch contains it. The code-only diff from `8f870a3` to this head is nine files, 669 insertions and 73 deletions (`git diff --stat 8f870a3 HEAD -- models/ralf/src models/ralf/tests`). In `modeling_ralf.py`, the `randperm` device adjustment is limited to label (`c`) and relation shuffles; the other changes harden relation IDs and select relation preprocessors. In `lightning_module.py`, `_model_batch` passes sample IDs for every condition, but `inputs.id` is read only by `_relation_ids`. `datamodule.py` requires and carries `sample["id"]` as identity metadata; `training_reference.py` passes vendor IDs from `sample_ids` for the relation adapter. `run_training_stages.py` adds relation branches and changes S3 workdir/cwd selection; it does not change completion model or loss logic. The remaining four changed files (`test_modeling_helpers.py`, `test_relation_table.py`, `test_training.py`, and `test_training_harness.py`) test those relation and adapter paths.

At current HEAD, `_training_workdir` in [run_training_stages.py](tests/vendor_parity/run_training_stages.py#L353) selects `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/<condition>/workdir` regardless of `--dataset`; a fresh-cache S3 rerun therefore requires the cache symlink tracked by [issue 421](https://github.com/creative-graphic-design/design-generators/issues/421). The S0-S4 records above were produced at `8f870a3`; create this link before rerunning S3 at HEAD:

```bash
mkdir -p .cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/completion/workdir
ln -s "$RALF_CACHE_DIR" .cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/completion/workdir/cache
```

Each final checkpoint used the original completion evaluation chain: `cond_type=partial`, `+sampling=top_k`, `top_k=5`, temperature 1.0, inference seeds 0/1/2, evaluator `--run-on-local`, inference/evaluation `num_workers=0`, and evaluation concurrency 1. Package and vendor training used 50 epochs and batch size 32. The authors' completion scores are marked reference only and are excluded from package/vendor tests.

This matrix accounts for each checkpoint condition separately, so an unrun condition cannot be mistaken for evidence from another condition. Each row identifies the dataset, system, status, seed scope, metrics, loss evidence, and retained artifact location. The CGL conditions have staged records; label, label-size, completion, refinement, and relation also have full-run evidence. PKU unconditional, label, label-size, completion, and refinement each have five matched package/vendor training-seed runs; PKU relation remains unrun.

### CGL Unconditional S5 Comparison

Each system trained seeds 1-3 to 30 epochs, and every resulting checkpoint was evaluated with the original implementation's own chain: its inference entrypoint with `cond_type=uncond`, `test_split=test`, and `top_k` sampling, followed by its `eval.py` with the authors' FIDNet weights. Package checkpoints enter that chain through the evaluator adapter, which removes the single Lightning `model.` prefix and reuses the seed-matched vendor `config.yaml`. Inference runs with `num_workers=0` because the entrypoint holds every generated batch until it serializes results, and dataloader workers would stage that accumulation in `/dev/shm` (measured 128 GiB in 40 minutes on this host); worker count does not change the outputs.

| Metric (test average)   | Package mean [min, max] | Vendor mean [min, max]  |

| Underlay (loose)        | 0.9925 [0.9920, 0.9934] | 0.9926 [0.9918, 0.9932] |

Every package mean falls inside the vendor three-seed range, which is the basis for the CGL unconditional end-to-end reproduction claim above. An independent rerun of the full vendor-parity test suite (`PARITY_REQUIRE=1 ... pytest models/ralf/tests/vendor_parity -m vendor_parity` with regenerated golden references and all twelve converted checkpoints) reported 28 passed, 0 failed, and 0 skipped. The per-seed score files (one YAML per run under each run root's `scores/` directory) have these SHA-256 values: package seeds 1-3 `ee58b8ac4d25437e111173c31ebce39c18c288d565c8b0222d357248e95ce985`, `2972dc798be946a8f0d073e8d18391121268d8e40e544ec50d98bd7e9d481577`, `6a26d8a0a86ec4d335ed06f0d863548b9bc7996d2c157ea653fc6fd428d0ab35`; vendor seeds 1-3 `c7e30d6171127a7d1d87c6b9b958d253bdabb5a8dbeadde646f0f649422539c6`, `1f90ceba3cdac32a8250432e1a3d2fbf50eabb690bffadb5ca39bba4abb22c8e`, `6113cedcc75854041ea693632c75f71dba0d42264eb9e46fa023cf10aef0ccc7`.

The authors' published reference is stored in the read-only RALF cache at `$RALF_CACHE_DIR/training_logs/ralf_uncond_cgl/generated_samples_uncond_name_top_k_temperature_1.0_top_k_5_final_dynamictopk_16/scores_all.yaml`, outside the repository `.cache/` tree. Its SHA-256 is `295f4a66eaf2920dad4f5745bdd82492dc93908cccbaa4f7704c1d87db152742`. The following values were recomputed from that file's `average.test` mapping and are reference only, meaning they are excluded from the package/vendor statistical test.

The authors' `config.yaml` records 70 training epochs, whereas the repository recipe followed 30 epochs from `vendor/ralf/configs/ralf_cgl/uncond.sh`. The cache also holds a `_debug_` variant of this directory; the values above come from the non-debug one.

S4 run-010 compared the actual package `RalfDataModule.train_dataloader()` and `val_dataloader()` with the vendor `DataLoader` and `collate_fn`. Train and validation split membership matched exactly (48,544 and 6,002 ids, respectively), with zero overlap. The package and vendor canonical stream digest was the same: `664a421335f288c1a3beafc92f53d5d88ba685e5d393aea8fcc6eb484cdf3876`. The loader digests were package `2ef173685465a5810cfcaed76f2788cba85c2f48c85b5a83229cf004be1b4f3e` and vendor `5970e735922f26706018496a90f4bf9cda40c6dea1dbde0f4e980b4446f92ab7`.

The corrected CGL S0 run-003 used the corrected 30-epoch recipe and passed with 44,386,946 parameters, 664 state-dict keys, and milestone 21. Its candidate source digest is `cf21004402441022542289debd19a6ac2d3cca5ad1fa3a8b406406222f921b6f`, its effective config digest is `be54c44b7e677a9aee75c7076ca2d20e04d4c56d58c3defc45ead4a452b93f61`, and its pinned vendor revision is `c51db6032acbd0bd0ce72433becce08317e7874d`. The recorded source digest identifies the dirty candidate tree used for this diagnostic; it is not a claim that the artifact was generated from a clean commit.

### PKU Unconditional S5 Comparison

Each system trained five matched seeds for 50 epochs with batch size `32`, training `num_workers=4`, and `OMP_NUM_THREADS=4`; each final checkpoint used the original PKU10 evaluation chain with `cond_type=uncond`, `test_split=test`, top-k `5`, temperature `1.0`, inference seeds `0/1/2`, evaluator `--run-on-local`, and inference/evaluation `num_workers=0`. The evaluator worker setting is scheduling-only; training retained `num_workers=4`. The primary table reads the `average.test` values from ten ledger-verified `scores_all.yaml` files. Package mean in vendor range means the package mean is within the vendor seed minimum-to-maximum range; vendor mean in package range is the reverse check. Welch p is the two-sided unequal-variance test. Exact permutation p enumerates all `252` relabelings and uses `Fraction(str(value))` arithmetic for every metric. The dataset was materialized from an internally preprocessed copy produced with the vendor's hfds_builder pipeline; ids verified against `data_splits/splits/pku` and `pku_DATAID_TO_IDX.pt`.

The candidate interpretation is qualitative-with-caveat: both-direction range agreement passes `9/15` metrics, while `R_{shm} (vgg distance)`, `occlusion`, `overlay`, `test_density_layout`, `utilization`, and `validity` fail at least one direction. No Welch or exact permutation p-value is below `0.05`. The largest absolute Welch-standardized mean difference is `1.969` on `utilization` (Welch p `0.085587904197962486`). The authors' reference is excluded from the package/vendor tests. Verdict: Unconditional PKU10 training is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation. No metric reaches p<0.05 under Welch or exact permutation (layout FID p=0.412698412698412698412698), and 9/15 metrics pass the both-direction range test; this is the weakest range agreement among the RALF sections, with the largest standardized difference 1.969 on `utilization` (Welch p≈0.0856). The authors' published checkpoint (layout FID 3.449) is below both the package and the vendor rerun means under the same 50-epoch budget, so the gap is not attributable to the package port.

Status: `s5-practical-reproduction`

### PKU Label S5 Comparison

Each system trained five matched seeds for 50 epochs with batch size `32`, training `num_workers=4`, and `OMP_NUM_THREADS=4`. Each final checkpoint used the original PKU10 evaluation chain with `cond_type=c`, `test_split=test`, top-k `5`, temperature `1.0`, inference seeds `0/1/2`, evaluator `--run-on-local`, and inference/evaluation `num_workers=0`. The primary table reads `average.test` values from ten ledger-verified score files. Both-direction range agreement requires each system mean to lie within the other system's inclusive observed range. Welch is the two-sided unequal-variance test. Exact permutation p enumerates all `252` relabelings with `Fraction(str(v))` arithmetic.

The metrics failing both-direction range agreement are `overlap-LayoutGAN++`, `test_density_layout`, `utilization`, and `validity`. The largest standardized difference is `2.192` on `utilization` (Welch p `0.060682696232903351`). No Welch p-value is below `0.05`; the one exact permutation p-value below `0.05` is for `utilization`, exact permutation p = `1/21`.

Verdict: Label-conditioned PKU10 training is reproduced within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation. 11/15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, no metric reaches p<0.05 under Welch, and one metric reaches p<0.05 under exact permutation: `utilization` (p=1/21, Welch p≈0.061, standardized difference 2.192, the largest in this section); layout FID has exact permutation p=0.5. The authors' published checkpoint (layout FID 2.2116014810520426) is below both means under the same 50-epoch budget, so the gap is not attributable to the package port.

Status: `s5-practical-reproduction`

### PKU Label-Size S5 Comparison

Can package label-size training reproduce the vendor's PKU10 results across five training seeds? We compared five package and five vendor checkpoints over 15 `average.test` metrics using the both-direction range test. The result is 13/15 range passes, with both underlay means above the vendor ranges. Exploratory two-sided Welch and exact permutation tests (training-seed n=5 per system): p<0.05 for 0/15 metrics under either test; these exploratory tests cover only these seeds, and a non-significant result does not show equivalence. The final classification is `s5-practical-reproduction`.

Each system used 50 epochs, batch size `32`, and training `num_workers=4`. Evaluation used `cond_type=cwh`, `+sampling=top_k`, `top_k=5`, temperature `1.0`, `test_split=test`, inference seeds `0/1/2`, `--run-on-local`, and inference/evaluation `num_workers=0`. The table reads `average.test` values from ten score files whose SHA-256 digests match their run ledgers. Both-direction range agreement requires each system mean to fall inside the other system's inclusive observed range. The standardized difference is `|μ_package − μ_vendor| / sqrt((s_package² + s_vendor²) / 2)`, where `s` is the sample standard deviation. Exploratory two-sided Welch is the unequal-variance test; the exploratory exact permutation test enumerates all `C(10,5)=252` relabelings using `Fraction(str(v))` arithmetic. The [PKU label-size reproduction issue](https://github.com/creative-graphic-design/design-generators/issues/308) prespecified no inferential test or threshold.

The only range misses are `underlay_effectiveness_loose` and `underlay_effectiveness_strict`. Exploratory two-sided Welch and exact permutation tests (training-seed n=5 per system): for loose underlay, Welch p=`0.17001022336647229` and exact permutation p=`11/63`; for strict underlay, Welch p=`0.090626588698922803` and exact permutation p=`13/126`. The largest standardized difference is `1.242016112906604` on `underlay_effectiveness_strict`, copied from the comparison artifact. Validity is `1.0` for every seed in both systems; both groups have zero variance, so its Welch p-value and standardized difference are undefined. The analysis script displays Welch p=`1` as a zero-standard-error convention.

Verdict: practical reproduction. Package training of the PKU10 label-size condition reproduces the vendor's training within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation: 13/15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, and the two misses, `underlay_effectiveness_loose` and `underlay_effectiveness_strict`, have package means above the vendor range with exploratory exact-permutation p-values of 11/63 and 13/126 (Welch 0.170 and 0.091); layout FID is 0.707 ± 0.126 (package) against 0.686 ± 0.052 (vendor), exact-permutation p = 5/6. Values are rounded to three decimal places (Welch p to three). Welch p and the standardized difference are undefined for the zero-variance metric `validity`. These exploratory tests cover only these seeds, and a non-significant result does not show equivalence. The basis for the status is the range test and the seed-to-seed spread; the issue prespecifies no threshold. The post-hoc 300-step lockstep probe at the launch commit c1a6ed2 passed and is accepted by the coordinator as the real-scale probe evidence; the deviation from the pre-launch ordering is recorded in the linked amendment comment on the issue.

Status: `s5-practical-reproduction`

### PKU Refinement S5 Comparison

The question is whether package refinement training reproduces the authors' published clean-target behavior on PKU10. We compare five package and five patched-vendor training seeds under the authors' 50-epoch, batch-size-32 recipe and original deterministic evaluation chain; 13/15 metrics pass the both-direction range test.

Exploratory two-sided Welch and exact permutation tests (`training-seed n=5` per system) find no p-value below `0.05` for these seeds; a non-significant p-value does not show equivalence. [Issue 308](https://github.com/creative-graphic-design/design-generators/issues/308) prespecifies no inferential test or p-value threshold. The range test requires each system mean to lie within the other system's inclusive observed range. The exact permutation test enumerates all `C(10,5)=252` relabelings and compares absolute mean differences using `Fraction(str(v))` arithmetic. The analysis script computes the standardized difference as `|Δmean| / sqrt((sd_p² + sd_v²) / 2)`. `test_coverage_layout` and `validity` have zero variance, so their exploratory Welch p-values and standardized differences are undefined.
