The authors' refinement reference score file `$RALF_CACHE_DIR/training_logs/ralf_refinement_pku10/generated_samples_refinement_name_deterministic_refine_mode_uniform_refine_offset_ratio_0.1_refine_lambda_3.0_final_dynamictopk_16/scores_all.yaml` has SHA-256 `ecf733a3c310612d5b7fcbcef4a18cf8c07709b231597a37079d42ebd926bc8c`.

S0-S4 ran at package source commit `8f870a3fd9ed8211e1adbf0d1042c2e23a97cd63` with vendor revision `c51db6032acbd0bd0ce72433becce08317e7874d`; the 300-step lockstep trace also records package commit `8f870a3`. Commit `8f870a3` is an ancestor of this branch, not the pre-rebase commit. The pre-rebase commit is `c101217059a77770b1a7f4f274eadb9496c17a64`; it is not an ancestor of `HEAD`, and `git branch -r --contains c101217` returns no remote branches. Per-run training records show package seed 1 at `8f870a3fd9ed8211e1adbf0d1042c2e23a97cd63`, package seeds 2-5 at `c101217059a77770b1a7f4f274eadb9496c17a64`, and vendor seeds 1-5 at package commit `c101217059a77770b1a7f4f274eadb9496c17a64` with vendor revision `c51db6032acbd0bd0ce72433becce08317e7874d`. The ten evaluation source-gate records resolve package source commit `c101217059a77770b1a7f4f274eadb9496c17a64`. `git diff --stat 8f870a3 c101217` reports only `models/ralf/TRAINING.md | 19 ++++++++++++++++++-` and `1 file changed, 18 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)`, so the pre-rebase-to-rebased difference is documentation-only.

The stable code-only comparison, `git diff --stat 8f870a3 HEAD -- models/ralf/src models/ralf/tests`, covers nine files with 669 insertions and 73 deletions. In `modeling_ralf.py`, the `randperm` device change is limited to the `c`/relation shuffle path; relation-ID handling is hardened, and refinement still selects `self.preprocessor`. `datamodule.py` now requires `sample["id"]` and carries sample IDs into batches. `lightning_module.py` passes `sample_ids` for every condition, but the model reads `inputs.id` only in `_relation_ids` (`modeling_ralf.py:957`), which refinement does not call. `training_reference.py` takes vendor IDs from batch `sample_ids`. `run_training_stages.py` adds relation-specific branches and changes the S3 vendor workdir and subprocess cwd. The other changed files, `test_modeling_helpers.py`, `test_relation_table.py`, `test_training.py`, and `test_training_harness.py`, add or update relation and harness tests. These changes do not alter the refinement training path.

Rerunning the PKU S3 command at current `HEAD` with a fresh cache also requires the CGL-named link because of the workdir bug tracked in [issue 421](https://github.com/creative-graphic-design/design-generators/issues/421). Create that link with `RALF_CACHE_DIR` set:

```bash
mkdir -p .cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/refinement/workdir &&
  ln -s "$RALF_CACHE_DIR" .cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/refinement/workdir/cache
```

At S5 startup, the campaign supervisor captured `source_commit=8f870a3`. Both the package-run and vendor-run conversion branches checked package source through `RALF_SOURCE_ROOT`, expected `8f870a3`, and found `c101217`. The check failed open ten times because both evaluation paths call `prepare_eval` inside an `if` condition; every failed check was followed by an evaluation launch. The manifest's `S5 conversion prepared … status=PASS` entries and its `source_commit=8f870a3` training entries are therefore not reliable provenance. Every `evals/*/conversion-source-gate.log` file is empty. The per-run `source-gate.log` files are the provenance of record.

Verdict: practical reproduction. Package training of the PKU10 refinement condition reproduces the vendor's training within the vendor's seed-to-seed variation: 13/15 metrics pass the both-direction range test, and the two misses, `test_density_layout` (package mean above the vendor range) and `utilization` (vendor mean outside the package range), have exploratory exact-permutation p-values of 31/126 and 37/126 (Welch 0.263 and 0.314); layout FID is 0.1246 ± 0.0114 (package) against 0.1251 ± 0.0191 (vendor), exact-permutation p = 61/63. Values are rounded to four decimal places (Welch p to three). Welch p and the standardized difference are undefined for the zero-variance metrics `test_coverage_layout` and `validity`. These exploratory tests cover only these seeds, and a non-significant result does not show equivalence. The basis for the status is the range test and the seed-to-seed spread; the issue prespecifies no threshold.

# Status: `s5-practical-reproduction`.

### PKU Completion S5 Comparison

Does package `completion` training reproduce the authors' PKU10 (ten-category PKU) result? We trained five matched seeds per system for 50 epochs at batch size 32 and evaluated each final checkpoint through the original completion evaluation chain described above. For this 5+5 seed sample, 12/15 metrics pass both-direction range agreement. Exploratory two-sided Welch and exact permutation tests (training-seed n=5 per system; these seeds only) find p<0.05 for 0/15 metrics; a non-significant p-value does not show equivalence. Issue 308 prespecifies no inferential test or numerical threshold. We propose `s5-practical-reproduction` as within the accepted per-dataset parity thresholds; the maintainer verdict is pending.

The table reports the test averages from five score files per system. Each directional range check asks whether one system's mean falls inside the other system's inclusive min-to-max range. Permutation values enumerate all C(10,5)=252 relabelings using Fraction(str(v)). The standardized difference is |package_mean-vendor_mean|/sqrt((sd_package^2+sd_vendor^2)/2); the analysis script computes it and emits it in the comparison JSON.

The three range-test misses are alignment-LayoutGAN++, overlap-LayoutGAN++, and test_precision_layout; in each case the package mean is inside the vendor range and the vendor mean is outside the package range. Their exploratory exact permutation fractions for these seeds only are 1/9, 23/126, and 41/126, respectively. Validity has zero variance, so its Welch p-value and standardized difference are undefined.

Verdict: [VERDICT PENDING MAINTAINER] Proposed: PKU10 completion training is within the vendor's observed seed-to-seed variation and the accepted per-dataset parity thresholds for this 5+5 seed sample. The package and vendor FID means are 1.84691612596104926 and 1.84476546029564274; 12/15 metrics pass both-direction range agreement. For these seeds only, exploratory two-sided Welch and exact permutation tests have p<0.05 for 0/15 metrics; a non-significant p-value does not show equivalence. The three range-test misses and their exploratory permutation fractions are reported above. The authors' reference FID is 1.5823874530880648 and is excluded from the tests. Validity has zero variance, so its Welch p-value and standardized difference are undefined. Issue 308 prespecifies no inferential threshold, so the maintainer decides whether this proposed status is appropriate.

Status: proposed s5-practical-reproduction

## Evaluator Adapter Diagnostic

The retained CPU diagnostic is a PASS for the vendor config, pickle loader, and validity checks, and is not S-stage evidence. The narrow adapter under `tests/vendor_parity` achieves that result by extracting the Lightning `state_dict`, removing the single `model.` wrapper, writing a vendor raw checkpoint beside its config, and mapping package `LayoutGenerationOutput` normalized `xywh` boxes, labels, masks, and explicit sample ids to the vendor pickle geometry fields. The retained JSON binds the reported PASS to the exact source checkpoint, raw checkpoint, config, and pickle; their SHA-256 values allow integrity checking.

| File                                                                     | Result or SHA-256                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/evaluator/run-001/evaluator.json` | PASS; contains the retained digest record                                                |
| Vendor raw checkpoint (`gen_final_model.pt`)                             | Included in the retained digest record                                                   |
| Vendor config (`config.yaml`)                                            | Loaded by `load_train_cfg`                                                               |
| Vendor pickle (`test_0.pkl`)                                             | PASS through `load_pkl` and `compute_validity`                                           |
| Vendor validity                                                          | PASS criterion met: validity `1.0` for both filtered samples; 664 state-dict keys loaded |

## Regeneration Metadata

The currently verified diagnostic environment is a pre-existing coherent cu128 runtime supplied through an externally managed interpreter, with torch `2.8.0+cu128`, on one selected Tesla V100-SXM2-32GB. The authoritative cache is supplied read-only through `RALF_CACHE_DIR`; all accepted CGL evidence references that environment variable. Current candidate metadata and retained artifact records carry the relevant source, canonical-config, and pinned-vendor digests needed to reproduce or audit the staged checks.

The currently selected authoritative cache is the user-provided local copy, selected only through `RALF_CACHE_DIR`; no shared-mount dependency is part of the rerun contract. Its content-role inventory is the CGL/PKU dataset trees, retrieval-index tables, precomputed ResNet/FIDNet/DreamSim resources, relationship and saliency data, evaluation features, and training logs. The per-file SHA256 manifest is `.cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/provenance/local-cache-manifest-001.sha256` (404 files; manifest SHA256 `17ce2b93df63ca8444b4be465f8b72d8a2ea9d2e480d98bb60f98de5a8150a96`). The manifest is generated from the authoritative local copy and is not committed.

The repository-default torch and CUDA metadata remain unchanged for the V100 host constraint. RALF declares its runtime `jaxtyping` dependency in the package metadata, and `uv.lock` records that dependency. To use the currently verified temporary interpreter, set `RALF_AUDIT_PYTHON` and activate its containing environment before using the member-scoped `uv run --active --no-sync` commands below. This is an observed verification condition, not a package requirement. The package and S0-S3 commands do not require `TORCH_FORCE_NO_WEIGHTS_ONLY_LOAD`. The S4 vendor loader command retains it because the pinned vendor path calls legacy `torch.load` without an explicit `weights_only` argument while loading cached retrieval/precomputed resources; it is scoped to that vendor-only boundary.

## Training Commands

Set the diagnostic paths explicitly before rerunning a staged check. The commands use one GPU and fail closed when local parity assets are unavailable.

```bash
: "${RALF_AUDIT_PYTHON:?set RALF_AUDIT_PYTHON to the verified diagnostic interpreter}"
: "${RALF_CACHE_DIR:?set RALF_CACHE_DIR to the authoritative read-only cache}"
RALF_AUDIT_VENV="${RALF_AUDIT_PYTHON%/bin/python}"
source "$RALF_AUDIT_VENV/bin/activate"
```

Run the focused package training checks.

```bash
PARITY_REQUIRE=1 uv run --active --no-sync --package ralf --extra training --extra vendor \
  --with pytest --with 'beartype>=0.22.9,<0.23' pytest \
  models/ralf/tests/test_training.py \
  models/ralf/tests/vendor_parity/test_training_harness.py -q
```

Regenerate CGL S0-S2 into new `.cache` directories rather than overwriting accepted artifacts.

```bash
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 PARITY_REQUIRE=1 \
  uv run --active --no-sync --package ralf --extra training --extra vendor python \
  models/ralf/tests/vendor_parity/run_training_stages.py \
  --stage S1 --dataset cgl --cache-dir "$RALF_CACHE_DIR" \
  --output .cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/<new-run>/s1.json \
  --steps 1 --batch-size 32 --seed 1
```

Use the same command with `--stage S2` for the one-step optimizer check.

For that rerun, set `RALF_GPU`, `RALF_CACHE_DIR`, and `RALF_S3_OUTPUT` as shown in the Stage Evidence table and run the complete command below. The run follows the protocol's layered S3 rule: the natural trajectory passes on its own when every step stays inside the S0-S2 contract, and otherwise the retained natural record plus a contract-internal synchronized layer is a bounded pass. The runner records the nested `traingen fit` command and its artifacts. The recipe supplies 30 epochs and the runner supplies the production limits, callbacks, logger, and cache paths.

```bash
: "${RALF_GPU:?set RALF_GPU to one selected V100 index}"
: "${RALF_CACHE_DIR:?set RALF_CACHE_DIR to the authoritative read-only cache}"
: "${RALF_S3_OUTPUT:?set RALF_S3_OUTPUT to a new repository-relative JSON path}"
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES="$RALF_GPU" PARITY_REQUIRE=1 \
  uv run --active --no-sync --package ralf --extra training --extra vendor python \
  models/ralf/tests/vendor_parity/run_training_stages.py \
  --stage S3 --dataset cgl --cache-dir "$RALF_CACHE_DIR" \
  --output "$RALF_S3_OUTPUT" --batch-size 32 --seed 1
```

Do not substitute a package model with the original model in the package Trainer.

Run the accepted CGL S4 loader-stream check into a fresh artifact directory.

```bash
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 PARITY_REQUIRE=1 TORCH_FORCE_NO_WEIGHTS_ONLY_LOAD=1 \
  uv run --active --no-sync --package ralf --extra training --extra vendor python \
  models/ralf/tests/vendor_parity/run_training_stages.py \
  --stage S4 --dataset cgl --cache-dir "$RALF_CACHE_DIR" \
  --output .cache/ralf/training-reproduction/cgl/s4/runs/<new-run>/s4.json \
  --steps 8 --batch-size 32 --seed 1
```

The 300-step real-scale lockstep probe evidence is recorded under [Stage Evidence](#stage-evidence), and the three-seed S5 training and evaluation results are recorded under [Reproduction Results](#reproduction-results), together with the independent vendor-parity test-suite rerun (28 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped).
